Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the graphs object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 882. The moduli space has dimension at most 513 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the graphs decomposition. By minimality, the element a^882 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 513-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
